                                      39
 1 09:57:01      probably?
 2 09:57:02  MR HICKEY:  I have a different view on termination and
 3 09:57:04      I think the reason for that can be expressed pretty
 4 09:57:06      simply.  There are two reasons.
 5 09:57:09          One, because clause 17 says so.
 6 09:57:13          Two, because ultimately -- and this is what is of
 7 09:57:17      paramount importance -- Qatari law says so.  It's not
 8 09:57:22      uncommon in all the Civil Code regions, just as it is in
 9 09:57:26      the common law world, that if there's early termination
10 09:57:32      of the works, people get paid for what's accrued, for
11 09:57:38      the entitlements that they have accrued up to that point
12 09:57:40      in termination.  There has to be a reckoning, and under
13 09:57:45      Qatari law, article 697, when the works are taken over,
14 09:57:49      you get paid your dues.
15 09:57:53          The effect of termination is effectively an early
16 09:57:57      take over of the work that has been completed to date
17 09:58:02      and obviously the rest of the work that has not been
18 09:58:05      completed and will never be completed under that
19 09:58:06      contract, just gets ignored.  You're not entitled
20 09:58:09      necessarily to get more unless you can prove a right to
21 09:58:12      damages, it depends on whether the termination is lawful
22 09:58:14      and all the rest of it.  But the fact of termination
23 09:58:19      brings to an end the parties' respective obligations
24 09:58:21      towards each other, apart from paying their dues for the
25 09:58:25      work that's been done.
                                      40
 1 09:58:27          So our case is that in this contract, when the main
 2 09:58:33      contract got terminated and our subcontract got
 3 09:58:35      automatically terminated, we are entitled to be paid our
 4 09:58:40      dues for whatever work has accrued up to that date that
 5 09:58:44      has been done.
 6 09:58:46          There is no more suspensory condition about that.
 7 09:58:48      It doesn't say that there's a suspensory condition on
 8 09:58:52      that in the contract.  It would be contrary to public
 9 09:58:56      policy if I have to wait indefinitely for many, many
10 09:59:01      years to be paid depending upon what disputes there are
11 09:59:04      up the line about termination and their entitlements in
12 09:59:08      due course.
13 09:59:09          That, in a nutshell, is our case, that termination
14 09:59:13      ends all and our accrued rights have to be paid for.

                                      80
 1 11:14:12      approach to liquidated damages, to damages -- and the
 2 11:14:16      term "liquidated damages" is simply there's an agreed
 3 11:14:20      amount -- you don't have to prove the actual loss.  You
 4 11:14:23      still have to prove a default by my client.
 5 11:14:25          The default is not that time has been missed,
 6 11:14:28      because the time has been missed for any number of
 7 11:14:31      reasons.
 8 11:14:33          The purpose of the extension of time clause is for
 9 11:14:36      their benefit not mine, because where you're making
10 11:14:40      a global claim for delay, you have to strip out from
11 11:14:44      that delay periods of time for which the employer or the
12 11:14:48      main contractor is responsible so that you can preserve
13 11:14:51      your right to claim liquidated damages.  Otherwise
14 11:14:54      you've infected the whole of your claim with something
15 11:14:57      for which you are at fault, and the law, the Qatari
16 11:15:01      law -- and I'll find the article in a minute -- requires
17 11:15:04      that the cause not be that there's no extraneous cause
18 11:15:08      that's beyond the control of the subcontractor.
19 11:15:11          So where there's a position whereby, for example,
20 11:15:14      there are variations or prevention access or untimely
21 11:15:19      approval of things, those are periods for which I should
